# Refract plan

The ordered work queue for Refract. It exists so one question gets a reliable answer: **what is next?**

- Last updated: 2026-10-28 (UI polish and every `design` issue raised to p1, 90 issues; earlier the same day: P2 triage with Stuart: 14 closed, 9 raised to p1, 57 lowered to p3, 7 narrowed, epics #1271 to #1273 created; listed under "Queue > Priority order"). Earlier, 2026-10-22 (working rule 13 director model and token budget; token-cost and worker-limit decisions recorded; earlier the same day: priority review with Stuart: p1 order set in "Queue > Priority order", 10 issues closed, 32 relabelled). Earlier, 2026-10-21 (#1113 round-2/round-3 gate follow-ups: #1115 `diff_stat.rs` symlink-following filed as its own issue; #1116 collects the remaining Low/Medium leftovers). Earlier: Rust promotion: epic #797 and lanes #784 to #796; earlier: the 2026-10-08/09/10 merge run, issues #443 to #650, plus long-open strays #71, #102, #388, #593, #603. **Phase R below runs first and changes how main-process rows are handled; its statuses were refreshed from merged PRs on 2026-10-14.** 2026-10-20: #1069 (Model roles overhaul) filed and placed after #714. 2026-10-20 (later): #1101 and #1102 filed, #730 and #1060 amended, from Stuart's Git changes panel screenshot feedback (away mode). 2026-10-21: #1069 built and live-verified on branch `feat/1069-roles-overhaul` (not yet built with #714, which has not landed); row updated, awaiting a build slot. 2026-10-21: #1119 to #1122 filed from a grill-with-docs session (Explorer tone, PR input height, PR checks RAG colour, one selected-item style); #756 amended (preview header consolidated into one ghost row with Source/Wrap, retitled); comments posted on #568 (superseded by #1121) and #700 (todo green, folded into #1118). 2026-10-21 (later): #1124 to #1126 filed from a grill-with-docs session (Menus: context/dropdown parity and muted icons, Tabs: accent underline no divider, Right-pane icon rail accent), placed after #1121; #1127 filed (Model roles reasoning radios follow-ups from PR #1114's final gate), placed after #1069. 2026-10-21 (later): #1069 merged (PR #1114, `55c77e93`); #700 slice (b) merged (PR #1118, `c8c30404`); #1151 filed (gate follow-ups from PR #1118's round-2 gate), placed after #700/#701.
- Source of truth for scope is the GitHub issue. This file holds the **order**, **dependencies** and **status**. Do not copy issue text here.
- Freshness marker: the **date above**, not a pinned commit. Re-check `origin/main` and the open issues/PRs each time this file is used -- a pinned sha goes stale the moment another docs PR lands concurrently, as happened between PR #607 and PR #608 on 2026-10-09.

## How to use this file

Stuart can say:

| Prompt | What the agent does |
|---|---|
| **What's next?** | First check for an open plan PR (`gh pr list -R stuarthopwood/refract --state open --search "head:docs/plan"`) and read that branch's `docs/PLAN.md` instead of `origin/main`'s when it exists, since it is newer. Then read the Queue top to bottom. Report the first row whose status is `next`, or the first `todo` row whose "Needs" are all `done`. List what can run in parallel (different files, see "File collisions") and anything blocked on a Stuart decision. Do not start work. |
| **Implement the next story** | Take that row, then **stop and confirm with Stuart** (working rule 9): name the story, give the approach and open decisions, and wait for his approval before creating a worktree or editing. After approval: read the issue and the bot triage comment (see AGENTS.md trust model). Work in a **fresh worktree cut from `origin/main`**, never in the main checkout. Implement through Spec Kit where the issue needs a spec. Run the quality gate. Commit and push the branch. Update this file (status, PR link) in the same change. Do not merge unless told. |
| **Implement #N** | Same as above for that issue, regardless of queue order, including the confirmation step. |
| **Update the plan** | Re-read the open issues and `origin/main`, then refresh status, order and the spec table. Say what changed. |
| **Why is X after Y?** | Answer from "Needs" and "File collisions". |
| **Create new issues** (any request to file or amend issues) | File them, then apply working rule 8: place each one in the Queue, update the issue index, and commit and push the plan. Report where each landed. |

Status values: `done`, `next` (start here), `todo`, `blocked` (needs a decision or a dependency), `in-flight` (someone is already on it, do not duplicate), `verify` (built, needs a live check and issue cleanup).

## Working rules (from Stuart, 2026-10-06)

1. **Issue-only unless told.** Filing and analysing issues never implies implementing. Implement only on "implement", "implement #N", "implement the next story", or an equivalent instruction.
2. **Decisions go through `ask`**, never buried in prose. Record the answer in the issue and in the Decisions section below.
3. **The first edit of any story is guarded (#387).** After every edit, check `git diff --stat`: only the intended lines may change. Before committing, scan the files you touched for odd-indent lines (the code base is strictly 2-space; an odd leading-space count that is not a JSDoc ` * ` continuation is the damage signature). Any whole-file churn means stop and re-do the write with a raw filesystem write.
4. **Doc edits and all work land on a branch cut from `origin/main`.** Never on `main`, and never in a checkout with unrelated changes.
5. Follow AGENTS.md: literal absolute paths only for deletes, commit and push after every logical change, answer the question first, and put those rules in every delegation brief.
6. After code changes run the quality gate (tests, build, lint, then reviewers). `main` needs typecheck and production build to pass.
7. Specs are the unit for large features. Small bugs and polish go straight to a branch.
8. **New issues update the plan.** Whenever Stuart asks for new issues to be created, or an existing issue's scope is amended, update this file in the same turn, before the final report:
   - Place each issue in the Queue: phase, position, "Needs", files touched, status. Fold duplicates into the issue that already covers them (as #11 into #440).
   - Add it to the issue index and record any decision he made in "Decisions".
   - Note which existing rows it blocks, gates or overlaps, and any new file collision.
   - Commit and push the change on a branch named `docs/plan-<yyyy-mm-dd>`. Start it from the newest **open plan branch** if one exists, otherwise from `origin/main`, so placements from an earlier filing turn are never lost. Then open a **docs-only PR** to `main` (or update the open one). **Standing permission (Stuart, 2026-10-07): the agent may merge PRs in this project when it sees fit**, so it merges a clean plan PR itself once its checks pass; after a merge the next filing turn starts a fresh branch from `origin/main`. A story's own PR must never carry plan changes except that story's status row; if that row conflicts, take the newer `PLAN.md` and re-apply the row.
   - This is the **one write permitted in issue-only mode** (rule 1). Report the placement in the final message, one line per issue ("#NNN is row C4, after #463").
9. **Confirm before starting a story.** On "implement the next story", "implement #N" or similar, do not create a worktree or edit anything yet. First state the story, the approach (Spec Kit route, files touched, how it will be verified) and any open decisions, ask for approval with `ask`, and wait. Start only after Stuart approves. A harness nudge or reminder is not approval.
10. **Electron main-process freeze ended at the cutover (Stuart, 2026-10-13 to 2026-10-15, Rust promotion, epic #797).** `src/main` and `src/preload` were frozen to P0 bug fixes only while the Rust build (`src-tauri/`) caught up; both directories, plus `electron.vite.config.ts` and the `electron`/`electron-vite` dependencies, were **deleted at R13** (#796), so the freeze and the "Rows re-homed to Rust" lookup no longer apply. `src/renderer` and `src/shared` continue unchanged, now serving the Rust (Tauri) shell only.
11. **No god files (Stuart, 2026-10-13, from a side chat).** Soft cap of **600 lines per source file** (TypeScript, TSX, scripts, Rust). A new file over the cap fails the gate; an existing offender may shrink, never grow (ratchet #806, `pnpm check:file-size`). Splits move code unchanged, one file per PR, with a live smoke run; see the God-file splits row in Phase D. Not applied to the Electron main-process files, which are deleted at the cutover.
12. **Grill first, then the full Spec Kit plan (Stuart, 2026-10-15).** Every story starts with a `/grill-with-docs` session (the main agent interviews Stuart against `CONTEXT.md`, `docs/adr` and the code, one question at a time with a recommended answer, updating `CONTEXT.md` and ADRs inline), and its outcomes feed the Spec Kit steps. Then the issue's whole Spec Kit pickup plan runs in order, with no step skipped (converge: amend the spec, `/speckit.converge`, `/speckit.implement`; specify: specify, clarify, plan, tasks, analyze, implement). The grill is run by the main agent with Stuart, never by a delegated worker (workers cannot ask Stuart questions); the main agent passes the outcomes to the worker in its brief. Put this rule in every delegation brief.

13. **Director model and token budget (Stuart, 2026-10-22).** Refract's October token spend was 77% one worktree (`earnest-stork`, the Rust-promotion lanes), from long-lived lane workers re-reading ~400k tokens of context on every call; output was under 10% of the cost. So: the main agent directs and does not do bulk reading itself; one worker per task (one issue slice, file group, review angle or gate round), never handed new work after it finishes; many small, tightly scoped workers beat two long lane workers; split any task over ~80 model requests or 120k tokens of context; pick the cheapest fitting agent (generic `task` by default, `scout`/`sonic`/`mechanic` when no judgement is needed, reviewers only for gates); a re-review after fixes is a fresh reviewer scoped to the fix. Run one lane at a time while the budget is tight. Put this rule in every delegation brief.

### Phase R: Rust promotion (P0, runs first and in parallel with Phases A to E)

**Stuart, 2026-10-13: the Rust (Tauri v2) build is promoted to be the main build.** Epic #797 holds the plan; each lane is a P0 issue. **Decided:** Electron is **deleted at the parity gate** (R13); the Rust build keeps its own data folder and **imports once** from `%APPDATA%\pinion`; Electron main-process features are **frozen** (rule 10); the **installer is a P0 gate** (R11). State on 2026-10-13: branch `feat/780-tauri-rust` (10 commits ahead, 2 behind `main`), **24 of 86 request channels** and the milestone-1 events done, about 7,700 lines of Rust against about 17,000 lines of Electron main and preload TypeScript; **62 requests and 12 events remain**; no CI exists for either shell; `parity.md` is stale; the worktree `C:/Repos/personal/pinion-worktrees/rust-tauri` has someone else's uncommitted edits (three files): **leave them alone**, do not commit or sync that checkout; R1 works from a fresh worktree off the last committed tip (`8d46ced`) and coordinates with the owner.

**Progress, refreshed 2026-10-14 (from merged PRs, not from the lane issues):** merged: R1 (#805, #816), R2 (#826), R3 (#827) plus its `terminatedUnexpectedly` follow-up (#851), R4 (#821), R5 (#845) plus its reattach/stream-eviction follow-up (#858), R6 (#860), R7 (#828), R8 (#822), R9 (#824), R10a (#818), R10b (#823, **not live-verified**), R11a (#819), R11b (#829) and R11d (#837) plus the release pipeline (#853) and the rename to Refract with installer icon (#855), R12a (#820), R12b (#834), R13a (#833) plus its debug-build import-isolation follow-up (#854), RM0 (#817), RM (#836) plus its follow-ups (#852, #857; plan mode remains a known gap). A cross-cutting Tauri frame-delivery fix (#859, fixes #856) also landed, stopping the settle-time `get_state` round trip from blocking frame delivery across the Rust agent chain. **Not started:** the live checks the gate needs (R10b, R11 update path, the whole R13 checklist). The critical path is now Stuart daily-driving (R13's trigger, decided 2026-10-14).

**Order (optimised).** Start the blockers and the risk first, then run disjoint lanes in parallel, then serialise only the one shared file.
- **Wave 0 (now, no dependencies, parallel):** R1, R12a, R10a, R11a, RM0.
- **Wave 1 (after R1 merges, fully parallel, disjoint files, none touches `agent.rs`):** R2, R7, R8, R9, R10b.
- **Wave 2 (serialised on `session/agent.rs`, one PR at a time):** R4, then R3, then R5 (after R2 and R9 so its chips are complete), then R6. The module split in R1 reduces the serialisation to a one-line hook each.
- **Wave 3:** RM (after R4 and R8), R11b (once the binary is stable).
- **Wave 4:** R12b then R13.
- **Critical path:** R1, R4, R3, R5, R6, RM, then the gate. Everything else overlaps it.
- **Dogfood threshold:** after R1, R4, R2 and R8 the Rust build is usable for daily work with Electron as the fallback; the rest can land while Stuart uses it.

**Dual-shell rules.** (1) `src/shared` and `src/renderer` are single copies; a new channel in `ipc.ts` shows up in `check:parity` as missing automatically. (2) No new main-process feature in TypeScript. (3) One agent per Rust file; the Files column is the ownership list; `ipc/mod.rs` and `session/agent.rs` change only through a one-line registration or hook. (4) Every lane PR updates the generated parity table, ports its named tests and records a live check. (5) A feature that exists only in Rust is acceptable: Electron is a frozen fallback.

**Rows re-homed to Rust (do not build these in TypeScript; the renderer half continues).**

| A7 | #599 | **Bug: Delete Session fails with "is not a working tree" and cannot be retried** when the worktree folder is left empty and unregistered (seen on `silent-orca`: empty folder, no `git worktree list` entry, branch still present). `sessions.delete` picks its path with `fs.stat`, then `removeWorktree` throws on any non-zero git exit, so the Session row, branch and Conversations are never removed. Fix: decide by `git worktree list --porcelain`; registered means `worktree remove --force`; unregistered: remove the folder only if it is a real directory (not a symlink or reparse point) under the worktrees root and empty (a lone `.git` file counts only if it is a regular file whose `gitdir:` points into this repository's `.git/worktrees`), no repo-wide `prune`, otherwise refuse with a clear message and remove nothing; then `branch -D`, Conversations, row. No Windows-lock handling unless reproduced first. Tests on a real temp repo for the folder states. #476 auto-cleanup depends on it. Two real bugs the live check itself caught and fixed: `gitChanges.stageAll` used to chain Changes+Untracked into one combined stage regardless of which group's own button was clicked (now takes a `group` arg); a new Rust-side `validate_pathspec` containment gate (`src-tauri/src/git/pathspec.rs`) added mid-slot, ahead of every pathspec this feature passes to `git`. **Gate round 1 (2026-10-20): FAIL -> fix -> PASS** -- a five-reviewer gate found 16 items: watcher lifecycle + busy-session guard on archive/delete/recreateWorktree, the ~2.2s liveness-ping refetch loop disabled for this watcher (`watch_directory_without_liveness_requeue`), NUL-delimited numstat parsing, no commit timeout (a hook may legitimately run long), a partially staged (`MM`/`AM`/`RM`) file rendering as two independent rows (Staged + Changes, like VS Code -- FR-002 amended), hardened git invocation flags (`--literal-pathspecs -c core.fsmonitor=false --no-color --no-optional-locks`), the store's shared `error` field split into `loadError`/`actionError` (FR-013 amended) so a background refresh can no longer silently clear an unacknowledged action failure, `load()` single-flighted with a coalesced trailing rerun and a generation guard (FR-015 amended), full focus restoration after every open-diff/Back/Escape/stage/unstage/bulk-action/commit (new FR-026, proven live via real `document.activeElement`), an unresolved conflict blocking Commit with "Resolve conflicts first" ahead of "Nothing staged"/"Write a message" (FR-010 amended), a Conflicts row's Stage action relabelled "Mark as resolved" and gated by a confirmation dialog (FR-006 amended), the diff pane showing Staged/Unstaged + refetching on live refresh + Escape-to-back (FR-008 amended), a real focus-ring bug (`outline-none` paired only with a width-only `focus-visible:outline-2`, never restoring `outline-style`), the Commit button moved to `aria-disabled` (was native `disabled`, which dropped focus to `<body>`), a commit-success announcement + subject-field refocus, capped/scrollable error and body text, every menu item/open-diff button naming its file, and `schedule_readout_update` wired into every mutating `gitChanges.*` handler (item 13: the composer's own "Changes" chip counts the worktree's diff against the Session's Base branch, not this panel's own uncommitted-status count, so a plain commit does not change its figure, only triggers a fresh recompute) -- all fixed, each amended into `specs/018-git-changes-panel/spec.md`'s own FRs. **Gate round 2 (2026-10-20): FAIL -> fix -> PASS** -- an external write to an UNRELATED file while a diff is open no longer tears it down or steals focus (the diff pane's own background refetch only shows `'loading'` for a genuinely NEW diff); Back/Escape from a partially staged file's own Changes-half diff lands on that SAME half's row (group-scoped lookup); a stale-but-known-snapshot background `loadError` is now a quiet always-mounted line distinct from the Not-available state's own alert; the commit draft moved into the store so the box survives a remount; a failed bulk Stage-all/Unstage-all no longer steals focus from the button just clicked. **Gate round 3 (2026-10-20): FAIL -> fix -> PASS, including a corrected false "flake" call** -- round 3's own first pass misdiagnosed a real product race (commit's success feedback -- clearing the draft, announcing, re-enabling Commit -- waiting on the WHOLE coalesced refresh loop, no upper bound while files kept changing) as a memory-pressure flake; a fourth review falsified that from source and found the real HIGH: `runAction`/`stage`/`unstage`/`stageAll`/`unstageAll`/`commit` now return their own `Result<void>` directly (never inferred from a re-read `actionError`), `commit()` fires its own refresh without awaiting it so its feedback lands the instant the commit itself succeeds, and `load()` itself now resolves after the next pass that starts after the caller's own call, never an open-ended drain. Also fixed: the Not-available alert no longer re-announces on every background refresh (Loading… only on the Session's first load); `commitDraft` genuinely survives a tab switch now (moved to its own map, untouched by `forgetSession`, proven live via a real tab switch); Back's row lookup falls back same-group -> any-group -> nearest-trigger; a failed single-file stage/unstage no longer steals focus; `min-h-full` on the diff wrapper so Escape fires after a click in the empty space below a short diff; `forgetSession` bumps the load generation so it can't be resurrected by an in-flight load. The live check itself gained two new checks (draft survives a real tab switch; commit's success feedback lands within 2s under sustained external file churn, live proof of the HIGH fix) and had its own Check 7b bug fixed twice this round (first a dev-only global confirmed absent from this build, then a DOM count badge that only renders with 2+ panels open -- both falsified live before being reported, not after). **Final slot run** (HEAD `67f068bd`, merged with `origin/main` first): two chained `pnpm ci:local` (16/16, `CARGO_BUILD_JOBS=2`) + `scripts/verify-464-git-changes.mjs` runs -- the first surfaced the two real gate-round-3 regressions above, each fixed and the full chain re-run clean -- **0 failures**, every gate-round 1/2/3 item proven live, never simulated; screenshots viewed by hand (`13a-draft-survives-tab-switch.png`, `13b-commit-during-external-churn.png`). The live CDP check then failed three times in a row on real bugs the first-ever successful full run surfaced (the `0 failures` claim above was never actually driven end to end before this round): (1) the transcript's stick-to-bottom gesture gate -- `event.nativeEvent.isTrusted` looked like a fix but is true for every browser-fired scroll regardless of cause, replaced with a `useScrollGestureTracking` hook (wheel/pointer/touch/scroll-key, ~500ms recency window, moved into `scrollLanding.ts` to stay under `Transcript.tsx`'s own cap), proved both directions live (wheel-away then streaming growth does NOT snap back; still-at-the-end streaming keeps following) via a two-delta tape split with a scripted hold between them; (2) `settleForScreenshot` threw on an infinite-duration (looping-spinner) animation's `.finish()`, now tolerant per-animation; (3) the E2 settle-reconciliation check raced a fixed sleep against the settle-time `get_state` round trip, which runs as a separate spawned task after the composer already re-enabled -- now polls the real DOM effect; (4) two Unpin-focus checks (P3, E6) raced `PinnedTodoPanel.tsx`'s own deliberately-deferred-one-rAF focus restoration with a single synchronous read, matching an identical case already fixed elsewhere in the same file -- both switched to the same `waitFor` pattern. **Final run, head `a2cbbe0b`: PASS, 0 failures, 0 notExercised** (`report.json`: `dirty: false`), five-theme screenshots viewed, 360/520px wrap reconfirmed. **Gate round 3 (self-caught, same continued session): two more fixes landed.** `scripts/verify-700-todo-pin.mjs` crossed the #806 600-line cap (603 lines) after gate round 2's own E4 rewrite and new focus assertions -- `settleForScreenshot`/`waitTwoFrames` moved into the existing `verify-700-todo-pin-helpers.mjs` (563/147 lines). A genuine (non-flaky) E4 regression then surfaced on a from-clean-HEAD re-run: the diagnostic geometry dump showed the current task's row sitting 14px below the list's own visible bottom. Root cause traced against the tape: `todo-5` sets Task 9 in_progress and the follow effect correctly follows it, recording its key in `lastFollowedKeyRef`; 300ms later `todo-6` blocks "Update docs" -- a row ABOVE Task 9 -- adding its own "Blocked: <blocker>" line, pushing every later row down without Task 9's own key changing, so the key-match guard silently skipped the recompute and left the row stuck out of view. Fixed by dropping the key-based early return (and `lastFollowedKeyRef` entirely) so the effect re-checks containment every render, gated only on real user-scroll intent (`userScrolledRef`) -- a pure read plus a conditional write already a no-op once in view, so nothing was gained by caching "already followed". **Final run, head `54623cb0`: ci-local 16/16 PASS, verify-700 PASS (0 failures)** (`report.json`: `dirty:false`), five-theme + 360/520px screenshots re-viewed. | **State management: XState** | **#706 (Stuart 2026-10-12): spike result on switching to XState, with `@xstate/store` only as a possible temporary drop-in for Zustand.** Verdict: **feasible, adopt selectively and incrementally, do not rewrite**: full XState (machines and actors) for the lifecycles Refract hand-rolls as flags (main start, stop and cancel lifecycle: 8 `SessionAgent` fields plus `OmpProcess` cancel flags plus `ReadyWaitController`; renderer per-pane actions; start-notice presentation; pane layout `empty / single / split`; queue chips), Zustand stays for flat UI state (right pane, view, theme, draft, scroll). **Evidence:** 22 Zustand stores, 159 hook sites, 61 non-React calls, 14 implicit machines; of 25 mined bug-and-workaround examples XState gives clear help in 11, partial in 9, none in 5 (#449 wrong invariant, #599 disk versus git, render churn, git locks). **External events: yes**, `emit` plus `actor.on` (synchronous, typed) in full XState, `enqueue.emit` plus `store.on` in the store. **Costs measured:** xstate plus `@xstate/react` about +14 KB gzip (45 KB min) over Zustand, 2.4 us per `send` (not for per-token data), 200 child actors 9.6 ms and 0.5 MB, `xstate/graph` cannot see timer-driven states or "late event after final", persistence restores context not `input`, Stately inspector understands actors but not `@xstate/store`. **Skip `@xstate/store` as a bridge** (as flat as Zustand, about 5x slower with one subscriber and 8x with 100, second package, no inspector); a 29-line adapter proves coexistence. Path: ADR, then main start lifecycle, renderer pane actions, pane layout and chips, per-Session actor system with emitted events as the IPC vocabulary, then decide on retiring mirrored stores. Four decisions for Stuart listed on the issue. Fixed by moving the scroll/settle logic into `Transcript.tsx`'s own `jumpToStageRow`, reusing `scrollLanding.ts`'s proven `runScrollSettleLoop`/stable-frames engine instead of threading new cross-cutting state; `stageJump.ts` kept only the DOM-id/context/margin constants `ChainStageLine` (a sibling of the Stage row, never its parent) needs to reach that callback. **Gate round 3 (live-check build slot, Main, item 5 "still streaming" case): falsified live that a jump made while the turn is still actively streaming holds for its own first settle window but can still be dragged back once a LATER Stage streams in afterward** -- JUMP-SPECIFIC, not a general scroller defect: a `requestAnimationFrame` scroll trace plus an instrumented-diagnostic comparison confirmed a REAL mouse-wheel scroll holds against the identical new content for a full 3s window, while `jumpToStageRow`'s own programmatic jump does not -- `@shadcn/react/message-scroller`'s own content-mutation-observer re-anchors to each newly-streamed item regardless of `autoScrollSuppressed`, and the library's internal `userScrollIntent()` signal a real scroll trips is not exposed via the public hook this codebase uses; two synthetic-dispatch fix attempts (a zero-delta and a real-magnitude `WheelEvent` dispatch meant to "signal user intent" the same way) were tried and falsified live (0 recorded events, no effect on the trace) before concluding this is not fixable from application code within this story's scope. **Decided (Skippy, delegated by Stuart 2026-10-19): do not block this PR on it** -- filed as a separate follow-up, **issue #1021**, and the live-check script records the still-streaming re-check as an honest finding either way plus a `notExercised` entry citing #1021, never silently passed, never hidden; the settled-turn proofs below (Stage 4 mouse, Stage 3 keyboard, the actual #1008 acceptance scenario) are unaffected and still hard-fail on any regression. **Gate round 4 (Main, code review + UI; a real product bug found, Test/A11y wording and precondition fixes)**: **M1 (Code/UI)** `shouldAbort` used to stop the settle loop silently -- no `onSettled`/`onInterrupted` -- leaving `landingInProgressRef`/`autoScrollSuppressed` stuck true forever once a jumped-to row left the DOM mid-loop, so `handleViewportScroll` and the ResizeObserver both went dead until an unrelated Session/Conversation switch reset them; fixed with a new `onAborted` callback (`scrollLanding.ts`) that `jumpToStageRow` uses to re-measure and release what it claimed, guarded by a `jumpTokenRef` a newer landing/jump bumps so a stale abort can never stomp fresher state, plus a `jumpCancelRef` every one of (new landing, new jump, "Scroll to end" click, unmount) cancels first so only one settle loop ever touches `scrollTop`. **M2 (Code/UI)**: the jump's own `onSettled` now records position (`useChatScrollStore`) the same way `onInterrupted` already did -- previously a jump that settled on its own left the scroll-memory store stale. **L1**: reworded two overstated "holds through every later streamed chunk"/"autoScrollSuppressed actually holds it off" claims (`Transcript.tsx`, `scrollLanding.ts`) to correctly caveat the still-streaming case per issue #1021. **Test/A11y/UI (reduced-motion wording)**: fixed in `scripts/verify-1008-stage-label-jump.mjs` (3 spots) to say `jumpToStageRow` has no reduced-motion branch at all (because `runScrollSettleLoop` never animates), not a now-nonexistent "instant-assignment branch". **Test Lows**: Stage 2's mid-stream phase now actually reads and asserts its own CLOSED state and a genuine before/after viewport contrast instead of only claiming it; the Stage 3 keyboard phase re-measures the off-screen precondition AFTER the jump button's own `scrollIntoView` instead of trusting the earlier, pre-scroll measurement. **Falsified live during the granted build slot**: the new Stage 2 before-check raced its own mount (Stage 1 runs alone first in this fixture) -- `aria-expanded` read `null` instead of `"false"`, a genuine hard failure on the FIRST run of the fixed script; Also added: a no-timestamp user message inserted directly before "yesterday" in the reopen fixture, exercising "the previous turn had no timestamp, so the divider can't be computed, so the label shows" (`showTimeGapDivider`'s own guard requires a real `at` on BOTH sides) -- "yesterday" now renders its own time instead of hiding; screenshot 05 now dismisses any stray hover card and scrolls the transcript to its own top first, so the oldest message is actually visible in the shot, not only asserted by the probes. **Self-caught regression during this round:** removing `role="separator"` broke the live-check's OWN divider-detection probe (it matched on that role), which briefly reported two false divider-placement failures against the FIXED app; re-ran once the probe was updated to match the new `data-slot` marker instead, confirming the app itself was correct throughout. `ci-local` 16/16 PASS at final git HEAD `66eaa8b` (every round). **Live CDP check PASS, zero failures, zero not-exercised** (`scripts/verify-555-user-message-time.mjs`, chained fresh build + run from the same HEAD): launch 1 (two human turns) -- both turns' `<time>` carry a real, fresh `at`, outside the bubble's box and visually above it; `mt-6` confirmed both BELOW turn 1 and ABOVE turn 2, turn 1's own wrapper (the first row) carrying no margin at all. Launch 2 (a GENUINE second app launch against the SAME `REFRACT_DATA_DIR`, not a same-process simulation, history reordered OLDEST FIRST, now SIX user messages): the FIRST historical message (a past year, no divider above it) keeps its own exact day-aware label ("11 Sept 2025, 15:44"); "this year" and "today" each sit directly under a confirmed-present `TimeGapDivider` and correctly render NO `<time>` row; "yesterday" -- immediately preceded by the new no-timestamp message -- correctly has NO divider above it and renders its OWN label ("Yesterday 12:00"), proving the previous-turn-no-timestamp case live; both no-timestamp messages render no `<time>` row and sit under no divider either. New `$conversationFile`/`$histTodayAt`/`$histYesterdayAt`/`$histThisYearAt`/`$histPastYearAt` sentinels added to `verify-tauri-fake-omp.mjs` (documented in the fixtures README, rewritten this round for the new 7-message/2-no-timestamp layout); the probe hardened so a missing row wrapper is its own error, never silently read as "hidden". Screenshot `05-relaunch-history.png` shows the full chain top-to-bottom: the first message's own label, the divider before "this year" (own time correctly absent), the no-timestamp message (no label), "Yesterday 12:00" rendered as a plain per-message label (not a divider -- no horizontal rule either side of it), then the divider before "today". Disclosed scope notes (PR body): launch 2's history is a canned tape, not data launch 1 persisted; launch 2 opens the Session via its existing session-list row, not literally the Conversation-switcher dialog, since `conversations.list` scans real files the fake omp never creates -- the wire-level reattach driven is the same code a switcher click would also exercise |
| **#560 quiet alert rows** | tracked in the row above (epic #549) | **Branch `feat/560-alert-rows`, draft PR opened 2026-10-16.** Built to the issue's own decision record (Skippy, delegated by Stuart 2026-10-16, `ui-architect-v3` roundtable). Two quiet single-line rows (`ProfileAlertRows.tsx`) render directly under the profile item, replacing `StatusBar.tsx`'s own low-memory and unverified-omp-version Popovers (its update badge is unchanged). Low memory keeps a "Triage" action, now opening `MemoryTriageDialog.tsx`: idle-only Sessions, a confirmed Stop per Session, and one "Stop idle" bulk confirm; no per-agent memory figure (no per-process memory API exists in src-tauri). Unverified version keeps a "Check" action opening the existing `HealthCheckDialog`: `ProfileAlertRows.tsx` mounts its own independent instance and `ProfileItem.tsx` keeps its own, each owning its own open state and focus-restore target (never lifted into `App.tsx`, which would have put `App.tsx` back over its own `check:file-size.mjs` baseline). Each row dismisses for the run only (local state, never persisted). Spec 001 FR-037 amended, new FR-072 added; spec 008 FR-029 now cross-references both. **Gate round 1 (four reviewers): 2 High findings fixed** -- `MemoryTriageDialog.tsx` gained busy guards (Escape/Cancel blocked, re-entry gated while a stop is in flight), a live idle re-check at confirm time, a latched confirm target/count (no more text flicker on close), a computed post-stop focus fallback (next remaining row's Stop, else "Stop idle", else the dialog's own focusable heading -- never a removed button), and `finally`-wrapped `stopAgent` calls; `ProfileAlertRows.tsx`'s announcements moved to the shared, always-mounted `useChatAreaAnnouncer` (the local region was silenced while the nav column is `inert`), joining both alerts into one announcement when they first appear together; its own padding is now conditional on a row actually showing; "N agents" pluralises correctly. **Live-checked** (`scripts/verify-tauri-560.mjs`, chained build+run, git HEAD matches the branch tip): both rows, Triage's confirmed Stop and bulk Stop idle, dismiss-for-the-run persistence across a memory-sampler tick, Check opening a real warning-state Health check, a keyboard-only Tab/Enter pass, and every accessible name -- all PASS, nothing not-exercised. **Gate round 2 (Code/Tests FAIL -> fix): three findings, Decided (Skippy, delegated by Stuart 2026-10-17), recorded on #560.** (F1) The round 1 focus-redirect fix mis-fired on an ordinary click anywhere with no focusable target: `document.activeElement === document.body` is true both when the row's own focused descendant is genuinely removed from the DOM AND after a plain blank-space click, so the latter also stole focus back to a row the user never touched. Fixed by additionally checking that the actual element leaving focus (`event.target`) is no longer connected to the document -- only a real removal causes that; a blank-space click leaves the same button sitting right there, still connected, so nothing redirects. Keyboard focus-return after closing Triage (a separate `onCloseFocus`/`onCloseAutoFocus` path) is unaffected. (F2) `stopAgent` resolved `Promise<void>` unconditionally, so a clean `{ok:false}` stop response (already toasted by the store itself) was announced via the live region as a SUCCESS regardless; in bulk, `Promise.allSettled`'s `status === 'rejected'` filter never matched a clean failure either, so a partly- or wholly-failed bulk stop always reported every target as stopped. `stopAgent` now resolves the real `result.ok` boolean (matching `archive`/`restore`'s own established pattern); both confirm flows announce the true outcome, naming the failing Session(s) by name where available, joined into one `announce()` call for a partial bulk result (two calls in the same tick would cancel each other). (F3) FR-037 still promised "Low memory ({N} agents)" on the row itself, but the round 1 "honest agent count" fix (`ProfileAlertRows.tsx`'s own item 5 comment) had already dropped it, since the system's live-agent count (idle+working+needs-you) and Triage's idle-only list can legitimately differ -- FR-037 reworded to match (the row says just "Low memory"), and `MemoryTriageDialog.tsx`'s own dialog description now actually states the idle count its comments already claimed it did. **Gate round 2 live-checked** (`scripts/verify-tauri-560.mjs`, extended; new `scripts/verify-tauri-560-gate2.mjs` split out, #806 cap): chained `ci-local.mjs` 16/16 PASS + this live check ALL PASS, exit 0, nothing not-exercised, together on the final HEAD `91f73e1` (`report.json`'s own `gitHead` matches). Confirmed live: F1's blank-space click leaves focus on `<body>`, never stolen; a keyboard Dismiss of the last row lands focus on the profile name button, never a disconnected node; F2's forced single AND bulk (n=1) Stop failures both announce "Couldn't stop {name}'s agent", never success, with the Session staying listed; F3's Triage description states the real idle count. Two further bugs found live and fixed, neither F1/F2/F3 but blocking a clean run: a WebView2/CDP input-timing click race on destructive confirm buttons (a synthetic click immediately after a dialog/AlertDialog opens is an intermittent one-shot miss in this harness -- confirmed via `app.log` showing zero second `stopAgent` IPC call ever reaching the backend when it struck, not a backend hang; settle-sleep guards added at every remaining site that lacked one), and issue #512's real low-memory start-defer feature (`spawned_in_flight() > 0` while `REFRACT_FORCE_LOW_MEMORY=1`) deferring a second concurrently-live Session's start for up to its own 60s ceiling, racing this script's own equal 60s wait -- the bulk F2 test redesigned to reuse the one idle Session rather than need a second. PR #945 body and evidence updated to this final run.

| **Queued messages fix** | **#540 queued message "Send now" can take two or more clicks** (bug, daily use). Cause from code: the chips flatten the follow-up and steering queues with no queue field, and both `sendNow` and `removeQueued` hard-code `queue: 'followUp'`, so a **steering chip can never be sent or removed**, a first click has no feedback, and remove-then-steer can lose the message if the steer fails. Fix: carry the queue on each chip, offer Send now only for follow-ups, use OMP's atomic `promote_queued_message`, add a busy state, and show a notice on failure. Do it together with #517 (both drop the `queuedImages` map). Probe `promote_queued_message` first | done (branch `fix/540-queue-chips-followup`, PR #598, merged; issue #540 closed) -- supersedes #591, which merged with the old client-side optimistic-reservation design -- that design is now fully replaced by a pure derivation function plus per-occurrence stable keys; `promote_queued_message` probed live on omp 18.8.0, FINDINGS item 46 | one agent on `MessageInput.tsx` (chip row), `SessionAgent.ts` (`sendNow`, `removeQueued`), `src/main/ipc/chat.ts`, `src/shared/ipc.ts`, `src/preload/index.ts`, `contracts/omp-usage.md` ("Send now"), `queuedChips.ts` at a time, with #517 and the Composer lane |
| **Enter sends queued follow-up** | **#1067 (Stuart 2026-10-20, UI polish): Enter on a blank composer sends the oldest queued follow-up (Send now).** Decided (grill-with-docs, all resolved; "blank" amended gate round 1, 2026-10-20): blank composer (`text === ''` exactly, not merely whitespace-only -- a space or a Shift+Enter newline is a real draft -- no attachments, no chips, no attachment read still in flight) plus at least one queued follow-up means `Enter` sends the oldest queued follow-up, same as its "Send now" chip, FIFO; a repeat `Enter` sends the next one. Queued steering messages are skipped (follow-up only); if only steering is queued, `Enter` on a blank composer is a no-op. Visible hint reads the shortened "Enter: send next queued" (gate round 2, fits 360px better; full sentence stays in the sr-only `aria-describedby` span) while the rule applies, Send button `aria-keyshortcuts`/tooltip never claim an action the disabled button can't perform. Reuse `handleSendNow` and its `inFlightChips` guard so a fast double `Enter` can't double-send; don't hijack `Enter` while the slash menu or IME composition is active, a ghost suggestion would accept on Tab, or the composer is `disabled`. `MessageInput.tsx` is at its file-size cap, so put the logic in a small helper (`enterSendsQueuedFollowUp.ts`, not a hook). Related: #540 (queued "Send now" chips), #744 (attachment-only draft is not blank) | **live-verified, ready for merge** (branch `feat/1067-enter-queued`, draft PR #1100; spec 001 FR-094 and tasks Phase 73 landed, amended for gate rounds 1 and 2). **FINAL LIVE-VERIFIED PASS** (build slot, Main, 2026-10-20): gitHead `189fd258`, dirty: false, 0 failures, 3 notExercised, all **33** assertions PASS -- includes every gate round 2 fix: CRITICAL focus-safety on every post-screenshot Enter; HIGH attachment-read-in-flight (`pendingAttachmentReads > 0`, "Attaching…" visible) **caught live** this run (the issue's own "both are checked live" wording holds, no correcting comment needed); MEDIUM whitespace-only draft and Send button `aria-keyshortcuts` both ways; LOW exact sr-only match. The table/code wrappers DO show horizontal overflow as expected but their own `scrollHeight` never exceeds `clientHeight` at any width, so the CSS overflow-x/overflow-y-coupling hypothesis (both wrappers set only `overflow-x`; computed `overflow-y` resolves to `auto` too per spec) is ALSO falsified by this fixture -- the coupling exists per spec but was never actually triggered.** **Gate round 3 (Main, time-boxed ~30min): probed two hypotheses the fixture still can't test. Proposal: about 12 px gap, then a slim right-aligned toolbar of two `icon-sm` ghost buttons (`ChevronsDownUpIcon`, `ChevronsUpDownIcon`, both verified in lucide 1.49.0) covering repositories **and** Archived, `aria-disabled` with a guarded handler and a reason tooltip when a no-op (never native `disabled`, which cannot take focus); **controlled** collapse state saved before first paint, new repository open, Archived closed, removed ids pruned. **Open: storage** (a settings field with a debounced `settings.setSessionListCollapsed`, recommended, or a flag on each `Repository`) and a **hidden-status hazard**: a collapsed header should show the top state of its hidden Sessions (needs you, then Unread response #731, then working) so a waiting Session is never invisible, recommended in this change. the toolbar's literal focus-ring class string (decision 23's own wording) drew no visible outline at all on `Button` (its base `outline-none` sets `outline-style: none`, which that class list never reset) -- fixed by building both buttons from the composer's own existing `COMPOSER_BUTTON_FOCUS_CLASS` fix (adds `outline-solid`) instead, as one new shared `SESSION_LIST_TOOLBAR_BUTTON_CLASS`; the first live-check driver's restart proof asserted a state identical to a fresh start's own defaults, so a build that persisted nothing would have passed it too -- the driver (`scripts/verify-tauri-735.mjs`) was rewritten to build a genuinely mixed, non-default state (one Repository closed, one open, Archived open with one Session actually still archived) and assert it both from `settings.json` on disk before quitting and from the rendered DOM after a real relaunch. **Gate round 2 (five-reviewer, 2026-10-15): one High (Closes vs Refs -- decision 29's Settings round trip and prune were still unexercised), fixed.** Added a third scratch Repository to the driver (empty, collapsed) and live-ran both missing checks: opening Settings and returning to chat (a full list remount) leaves the mixed collapse state untouched; removing that Repository actually drops its id from `settings.json` on disk without disturbing the rest of the saved state. Also fixed: a dropped Session create now fails the check instead of passing (row-count-aware wait, not composer-enabled alone); the focus-outline check now settles 250ms and asserts the EXACT 2px solid value (round 1's "3px" reading was mid-transition, not a real gap); both toolbar buttons get a REAL second click asserted to change nothing, not just a read of `aria-disabled`; a new `assertExeMatchesProcess` actually checks the launched process's own exe path and hash, not only logging it; the toolbar's no-op state now dims only the glyph (`[&_svg]:opacity-50`), keeping the focus ring at full contrast, and its disabled-hover resets are toolbar-specific and `dark`-prefixed (the composer's own reset lost a specificity tie and leaked the inherited text colour here); `init-stores.ts`'s prune now awaits restore before running, fully closing the D18 departure; Rust's `collapsedRepositoryIds` parsing is now one shared, capped (1,000 ids, 256 bytes each), `HashSet`-deduped helper used by both the disk loader and the IPC setter; `contracts/ipc.md` and `check-parity.mjs`'s `SETTINGS_SET_SESSION_LIST_COLLAPSE` lane corrected; the last two stray decision-number citations fixed. **Accepted Low departures, recorded on the issue:** a restored or created Session's group auto-opens even on a path where focus does not move, for example from the Manage sessions screen (D20) -- moving that call out of the shared `sessions-store.ts` action would touch `useSessionRowActions.ts`, which #811's own split review flagged as having only ~21 lines of headroom under its file-size cap. **Live evidence** (`scripts/verify-tauri-735.mjs`; a scratch Tauri instance, real CDP input, exe hash asserted against the launched process's own exe path): two Repository groups starting open; a Session archived while still the focused pane leaving no cue on its own former Repository header (only on Archived's, correctly); the toolbar's focus outline rendering at the exact decision-23 value (`outline-style: solid`, `outline-width: 2px`, `outline-offset: 2px`) under real keyboard focus; Collapse all/Expand all closing and opening every group plus Archived together, each with a REAL second click proven to change nothing; open-on-create and open-on-restore; the Settings round trip and the prune (decision 29); and a genuinely MIXED collapse state (Repository A closed, Repository B open, Archived open with its one archived Session still listed) surviving a real app quit and relaunch, proved both from `settings.json` on disk and the post-restart DOM. | **Start prompt never shows (investigate)** | **#985 (Skippy, filed 2026-10-18 from the #672 live check, PR #978): a slow agent start never shows the "Keep waiting" prompt in live Tauri runs (start-progress reached `waiting` but never `prompt` after a 150s fake-omp delay, prompt due at 60s); a third concurrent start never showed "Waiting to start" within 20s in three attempts.** Root cause found and confirmed resolved 2026-10-18 (same day): the live-check harness (`verify-672-start-message.mjs`) never built/PATH-prepended the recorded-frame omp shim, so every run silently launched the REAL omp instead of the fake one -- not a product bug. Fixed in the harness (commit `06f562d`, merged PR #978); that run (gitHead `2fa7973`) proves `prompt`/`extended`/`queued` all reach live for real, 0 failures. **A second, real gap found live the same night**: `sessions.list()`'s own `summary_fields()` (`session/agent/mod.rs`) never included `startProgress`, so ANY unrelated `notify_list_changed()` elsewhere in the app (repository rename, archive/restore/delete/worktree-reset, PR-link, another Session's own open/recover) made the renderer's wholesale `refresh()` wipe a starting Session's row -- `queued` never self-heals (no periodic re-emit, unlike `waiting`/`prompt`/`extended`/`memory-deferred`), so this one is a permanent wipe, not a flicker. Fixed in PR #988 (`summary_fields` now reads `state.start_queue.to_wire()` under the same lock `status_event_locked` already uses). | **Large paste loses its text (CRLF chip label)** | **#1235 (Stuart 2026-10-27, Bug, grilled, none deferred, `p1`; amended same day): a pasted block over the chip threshold with Windows CRLF line endings sends only its literal label, and the pasted text is lost.** The trigger is any keystroke after the paste, not the paste itself: sending straight after pasting works. `buildChipLabel` splits the first line on `\n` only, so the label keeps a `\r` that the textarea normalises away; the next keystroke's `onChange` copies the normalised value into state, the label then never matches the draft, the chip is pruned and nothing is substituted on send. Proposal: split the label's first line on CR or LF; chip text stays byte-for-byte | in progress (lane `fix-chat-crlf-paste-1235`) | none | `src/renderer/features/chat/attachments.ts` (`buildChipLabel`) |

Round 4 fixed six harness-only issues found re-running to completion (glyph-free sample bled into the pill's shadow; two `oklch()`-vs-`rgb()` parsing gaps; a documented CDP/Radix stuck-tooltip limitation; an unconditional Escape clearing the draft on one theme; the Send-click test's `/btw` never reaching `chat.send`, plus the fixture's own missing `prompt` response ack). Round 5 (test review + UI review): `--composer-selection-bg` made translucent and applied unconditionally (`COMPOSER_SELECTION_CLASS`, split out of the pill-only masking class) after the opaque fill was found invisible over a selected pill in two presets; a new pixel-level ink-visibility check (bright-pixel count, pill AND plain text, before/after Ctrl+A) that the old background-only check could never have caught; the live check's chip-clickable assertion retargeted from the real above-composer strip to the actual in-composer chip mirror, with a real dispatched click; Obsidian's pill-text chroma brought inside sRGB gamut; a literal `selection:[-webkit-text-fill-color:...]` placeholder in a comment that Tailwind's content scanner was compiling into a genuine junk CSS rule, reworded away (confirmed absent from the built CSS); and a #806 file-size ratchet violation (an earlier round had improperly grandfathered `theme-contrast-pairs.mjs` back over its cap via a manually-added baseline entry) found and reverted.
