| 012 Brand watermark (#406) | built (`watermarkEnabled` in settings, IPC, preload, `App.tsx`) | `verify`: of 18 task issues, 12 are closed (T001-T010, T012, T017, all code and docs confirmed on `origin/main`); 2026-10-30 `scripts/verify-406-watermark.mjs` passed 29 of 29 live checks on a debug build of main after #1364 (single pane, split, Settings, toggle, restart and hand-edited settings persistence, forced-colors and reduced-transparency emulation, click-through, drag-select, composer growth, resize, dividers, Manage sessions); T011, T013, T016 and T020 are ticked; T015 and T018 stay open for the human checks (Narrator, real High Contrast, Windows transparency toggle, listed under T015) and a release `pnpm build` in `specs/012-brand-watermark/tasks.md` -- T011/#418, T013/#420, T014/#421 (audit inertness, forced-colors, extend the contrast script -- corrected here: not actually closeable without a live pass, unlike an earlier draft of this row claimed), T015/#422, T016/#423, T018/#425 |
| 016 Rust (Tauri v2) build (#780) | **the architecture spec for Refract's only build (Stuart 2026-10-13, ratified by R13/#796, 2026-10-15)**; branch `feat/780-tauri-rust`, specs in `specs/016-tauri-rust-build/`; 96 of 96 requests and 23 of 23 events done; spec.md Status now reads "Ratified as the architecture spec" | R13 (#796) landed the parity gate and cutover: Electron deleted, the constitution amended (Principle V and the Technology section), spec 016 is now the sole architecture spec |

The 003, 008, 011 and 012 cleanup is cheap and can run as one read-and-verify agent per spec. It does not touch product code.

## Gated by #474 (do not build before the icon and button rules exist)

#469 tool icons, #466 / #467 / #468 message actions, #471 context menus, #472 conversation list, #463 / #464 / #465 / #470 panel features, #1 and #3 panes, #478 Jira panel, #457 toggles, and the Theme lane.

Bug fixes and logic-only work are not gated.

Not gated: #482 (it reverses an earlier status-icon decision and only needs A3 first). The FDB placeholders (C1) use only the shell and standard components, so they go ahead with the grill running in parallel, and are restyled if the rules change.

## Probe batch (B2)

One agent, read-only. The supported OMP range is moving to a **minimum of 18.6.3** (decided 2026-10-07, not yet applied to README, `docs/design/` or the constitution); the existing results below were recorded on 18.4.x and 18.5.x and are **historical** until re-run. Run the new items 14 to 19 on 18.6.3 or later, which is **unverified** until #520 runs. Record each result as a FINDINGS row.

1. `set_model` and `set_thinking_level` over RPC, including mid-turn and clamped levels (#454).
2. `omp config get|set|list --json`: can `modelRoles` be set as a record or only per key, do running RPC sessions pick up the change live, and what does `overriddenBy` report (#455, #475, #479).
3. Does a literal `@path` inside a prompt `message` expand over RPC (#465).
4. `runEphemeralTurn`: structured result channel, whether it sees the Session context, multi-turn behaviour, token cap (#1).
5. `/pause`: any route to the process-global gate over RPC or extension (#477).
6. `set_ask_dialog`: the structured `ask` request with `recommended` and `timeout` (#446, #444, #479).
7. `omp commit` headless, and the `commit` role (#464).
8. PTY on Electron 44.5.1: `node-pty` or a ConPTY wrapper through `koffi` (#3).
9. Code autocomplete route for the editor (#470).
10. OMP host tools: `set_host_tools` and `host_tool_call` round trip (probed on 18.4.x and 18.5.x so far; re-run on 18.6.3), so Refract can expose FDB Knowledge and Agent Builder to agents without an MCP entry (#392, #391).
11. FDB discovery (needs Stuart and the FDB owners, not an OMP probe): does the knowledge base have an MCP server (tool list), a REST or GraphQL API (OpenAPI), and what auth; the same for the Agent Builder (#392, #391). Also whether the `fdbrp-sme` MCP server is the knowledge base or something else.
12. OMP `/shake` and compaction: what `/shake`, `/shake elide`, `/shake images` and `/shake thinking` print, what each removes and whether it is recoverable, whether the manual command emits `auto_compaction_*` frames and reports freed tokens, and whether shake or compaction lowers `get_state().messageCount` (relevant to #449's reattach verification) (#506, #441, #449).
13. OMP `session_exit` records: are the `reason` and `kind` values (`sighup`, `dispose`, `pendingToolCalls`) stable across 18.4.x, 18.5.x and 18.6.3, what other reasons exist (SIGTERM, SIGINT, crash), what a Job Object kill writes (expected: nothing), and what an unmatched tool-start record looks like after resume. Also time how long Refract takes to open a 48 MB Conversation with `get_messages` versus `get_messages_page` (#510, #509, #449).
14. OMP native `btw` over `rpc-ui` (#516): does `btw` work in `rpc-ui`, do `btw_delta` and `btw_record` frames match `omp://rpc.md`, does a side question during a streaming turn leave `get_messages` unchanged, and what does `get_btw_history` return after a restart.
15. OMP `abort_and_restore_queue` (#517): queue two follow-ups and an image, Stop, check the returned `steering` and `followUp`, that no new turn starts, and that `remove_queued_message` returns the images.
16. OMP 18.6.3 `switch_session` with a saved model that cannot be restored (#515): error text, whether rpc-ui falls back with a warning, whether `provider` and `modelId` bind and skip the check.
17. OMP `get_state` `usageLimit`, `slowModeSupported`, `slowModeEnabled` and `set_slow_mode` (#521): shapes, when they appear, effect on Copilot and OpenAI models.
18. OMP wire schema (#519): is `rpc-wire.schema.json` or `rpc-wire.generated.ts` present in the installed binary or only in `@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent`, and which supported versions ship it.
19. OMP `set_event_filter` with `messageUpdates: "delta"` (#518): compare main-process CPU and bytes on a long streamed answer, and which delta event kinds the transcript builder must handle.
20. `gh` PR state data (#529, #499): **mostly answered 2026-10-07 on gh 2.102.0 against cli/cli**: `gh search prs --json` has `isDraft`; its `state` is `open`, `closed` or `merged`; no `--state` returns all three; `--state closed` returns closed and merged on both `gh search prs` and `gh pr list`. **Still to probe:** the minimum gh version Refract supports (older gh may lack the `merged` search state or `isDraft`), what the running app shows for a merged and a draft PR on each tab, and whether the per-row `gh pr view` check fan-out on the search tabs can be replaced by `gh pr list --search`.

## Decisions (Stuart, 2026-10-06)

- Explorer and git panels live in the **right-hand panel**, not the left Session list.
- **Rust promotion (Stuart, 2026-10-13, epic #797).** The Rust (Tauri v2) build becomes the main build. **Electron is deleted at the parity gate**, not kept alongside. The Rust build keeps its **own data folder** and does a **one-time, read-only import** from `%APPDATA%\pinion`. **Electron main-process features are frozen** (P0 bug fixes only; new main-process features in Rust only; renderer work continues). **The installer is a P0 gate** (spec 006 rebuilt on the Tauri bundler).
- Terminal: **per Refract Session, several tabs each** (each Session has its own set in its worktree).
- Default Dark theme lightening: **somewhere between** x1.15 and +0.15, background about `#1a1a1a` to `#1e1e1e`, chosen by eye. Divider strength reading still open.
- Scroll to top: **top of the conversation**. Scroll to your message: **the prompt this response answers**.
- #474 is top priority. #447 comes first for daily driving. Right-panel build order, **confirmed by Stuart**: shell and FDB placeholders first (C1), then the side chat (C2), then the Agents panel (C2c).
- **Side chat: native `btw` RPC only** (Stuart, 2026-10-07). Decision only: at implementation time the bundled extension is dropped, constitution II **will be amended**, and the **minimum supported OMP version will be 18.6.3** (18.4.x and 18.5.x support ends). None of those edits has been made. The probe (item 14) still runs before building.
- **Code blocks get a Copy button on every code surface** (Stuart, 2026-10-07, #524): markdown fences, tool Arguments and Output, write previews and approval details, **plus edit diffs, PR change diffs and the pasted-text preview**. It reverses the "No copy button" line, now updated in `docs/design/product-chat.md`. Diff copy semantics are still open.
- **Composer snippets: option B** (Stuart, 2026-10-07, #525): the composer stays a textarea with a live preview of the snippet; typed backticks are rewritten to a clean fenced block when the pair closes; on send the text may be normalised to a proper fence with the detected language tag. No rich editor.
- **Session row status line** (Stuart, 2026-10-07, #526): shown while the agent is working or waiting, **replaces the branch line** (the branch stays in the hover card), **Plain by default with a Plain/Playful Settings option**; needs-input and subagent lines stay factual. Who writes the playful phrases is open.
- **Session list headers** (Stuart, 2026-10-07, #527, #528): Archived Sessions move into **one group below all repositories** with a header styled like the repository headers (each archived row keeps a visible repository label); every group header gets an icon (Git repository, Archived), with remote and local-only repositories told apart (three states: none, known host, other remote). **Brand marks (GitHub, GitLab, Bitbucket) come from `@tabler/icons-react`**, imported per icon; lucide stays for everything else; Nerd Fonts rejected as a UI icon source (Private Use Area glyphs show as boxes when absent, manual screen-reader handling, filled style mismatch; no Nerd Font is bundled as an icon source). No checked library has an Azure DevOps mark. Open: sub-group or flat list inside Archived, generic and local-only icons, self-hosted remotes. **Revised 2026-10-09 (Main, delegated by Stuart, during #528's build):** no new dependency this round -- `@tabler/icons-react` is deferred (still the decided brand-mark source per the 2026-10-08 icon-set decision below, only postponed, not reversed), along with the three-way host classification; ships `lucide`-only (`FolderGitIcon`/`FolderGit2Icon`), a binary remote-vs-local-only distinction plus the same honest `unknown` case. #527 shipped flat (not sub-grouped). The deferred brand marks, three-way host split, and a richer host/owner/repo tooltip are tracked as **#646**, a follow-up to #528; the remaining open items above are superseded by that issue.
- **Bundled fonts** (Stuart, 2026-10-07, #4): ship more fonts with the app for both UI text (App, Chat) and Code, alongside installed system fonts in the same picker. This amends the old `docs/DESIGN.md` Fonts rule, now in `docs/design/product-look-and-feel.md`. A starter set from the measured candidates (Inter, Atkinson Hyperlegible, IBM Plex Sans, JetBrains Mono, Fira Code, Geist Mono) is about 223 KB of Latin-subset WOFF2. **The code fonts have no Nerd Font glyphs** (checked: 0 Private Use Area code points in the JetBrains Mono, Fira Code and Geist Mono Latin subsets), so #4's Nerd Font requirement needs a bundled symbols fallback or must be dropped. Open: the first set, default font (bundled or system stack), latin-ext, the symbols fallback, and the per-font OFL notices for the installer (spec 006).
- **Pull requests** (Stuart, 2026-10-07, #499, #529): a **Closed** filter, shipped with the #499 State control (not a sixth tab); **Closed includes Merged**, as on github.com; PR status pills are solid and coloured like GitHub's (Open green, Draft grey, Merged purple, Closed red) with the matching icons.
- **Pull requests list** (Stuart, 2026-10-16, #499 amended, #930 filed, from a GitHub Copilot screenshot): inactive Filter tabs are ghost and the active one gets a subtle background, in one row that scrolls sideways; the first tab is renamed **All** and the State control (default Open) narrows every tab; search and State move into a Filters section opened by a pinned ghost Filters button beside Refresh, and stay applied when it is closed (the button shows a dot); the list column is resizable (280px to half the view, default 320px, remembered) and the Project selector fits it.
- **App updates** (Stuart, 2026-10-09, #117): wants Refract to show when a new version is available and to update when he clicks Update. This is the existing feature, not a divergence: **already specified** as spec 006 User Story 3 (FR-012 to FR-016: startup check and Check for updates button, badge with version and release-notes link, Update downloads and verifies the checksum, installs on the next normal quit; tasks #309, #314, #322, #327, #331, #325, #262) and **already partly built on `spec-006-impl`** (stalled, see the spec tracks table). No new issue filed. There are no GitHub Releases yet for the check to find; spec 006 US5 allows a manual release as a stand-in, so #341 (publish workflow) is not a prerequisite for building and testing, and #460 (versioning policy) is needed before the first formal release.
- **Composer hint** (Stuart, 2026-10-07, #533): the empty composer shows an **icon overlay** hint (slash for commands, Markdown), chosen over the simpler native-placeholder text. This **widens the #528 Tabler decision**: `@tabler/icons-react` supplies brand marks **and** the Markdown icon, imported per icon; lucide for everything else (the #474 grill limits Tabler to brand marks; whether the Markdown icon stays is open, see `docs/design/foundations-icons.md` Open). **Superseded (2026-10-09, #533, PR #644):** the Tabler Markdown icon is dropped; the composer uses lucide `TypeIcon` instead. At the time, `@tabler/icons-react` stayed limited to GitHub/GitLab/Bitbucket brand marks under the 2026-10-08 "Icon set and style" rule (item 9 below); that brand-marks-only limit was itself superseded 2026-10-10 (see the new "Icon set and style — superseded" bullet above), though this specific icon choice (lucide `TypeIcon` over Tabler's `IconMarkdown`) is unaffected, since lucide already has the icon needed. `docs/design/foundations-icons.md`'s Open item is resolved, not left open.
- **Subagents pill** (Stuart, 2026-10-07, #536): the pill counts **running** subagents only; when none are running but some finished it shows a quiet **"N finished"** so the panel stays reachable. This amends spec 004 FR-007 ("running or finished"), to be edited with the fix.
- **Right pane rules** (Stuart, 2026-10-07, #538): all right-hand panels sit inside one rail and pane; the pane can be collapsed or hidden; every panel can be closed; two or more open panels get a tab bar; an open pane with no panel open shows a **centred list of available panels**, each with an icon and, where it has one, an active count with an icon and colour (agents running; git changes **green for additions, red for deletions**). **The icon rail stays** and **tabs also show the live counts**. Supersedes #394's "closing the last tab closes the panel". Open: counts on the rail icons when collapsed (proposed), persistence of open panels and width, a toggle shortcut, new spec vs amending specs 004 and 005.
- **UI refresh from the AI Studio mockup** (Stuart, 2026-10-07, epic #549): **dark presets only** (retune Dark and Dark high contrast; add Nordic Frost and Cyber Amber; **no light theme**); **ship the mock's palette as a new Obsidian preset** after correcting its contrast figures (Dark keeps its own retuned palette, decided 2026-10-08); chat width is a **Setting, default full width** (FR-049 stays the default); **soft decorative dividers with strong (3:1) control edges**; the blocker callout gets **one honest action** ("Ask agent to address this"; no Auto-Synthesize Fix, no Override gate); the mock **feeds the #474 grill before anything is built**. Open: canvas darkness, indigo as brand accent, the high contrast treatment.
- **Mockup review answers** (Stuart, 2026-10-07): **composer** gets the mock's thin status line with the **Approval mode pill inside the box** and the pickers in the box's bottom row (#564); the **Ask card** adopts the mock's active card with **tick boxes for multiple choice and a tab row for sequential questions** (#572); "**the top menu**" means the **right panel tab header** (comment on #538); **footer warnings go directly under the profile card** (#560); a **blocker is expanded by default** and its action **sends** the advisory as a follow-up (#558); the **DAG is loved** (#563) and **phase grouping is an idea to consider** (#571).
- Conversation titles: derive from the first message now, rename later (#440).
- The two FDB panels are named **FDB Knowledge** and **Agent Builder**. Both are **important and demo-critical** ("what is really going to sell this prototype to my bosses"): #391, #392 and #393 are `p1`. Placeholders (#393) first, real integrations after discovery.
- Session status style (#482): **colour-coded icons by default**, with a Settings option for **dots** and a preview of both.
- Switching Sessions and Conversations lands at the **end** of the conversation by default, and **restores the previous position if the user was reading earlier text** when they left (Stuart, #485 and #459). This partly amends spec 001 line 148 (restore only when detached from the end, not always). "Reading earlier text" means scrolled away from the end when leaving. **Amended (approved 2026-10-07, Stuart)**: the restore-when-detached rule applies only to a Session switch (the same Conversation, revisited); a Conversation switch (opening or starting one) always lands at the end unconditionally, since a Conversation just opened/started has never been "left" by the developer and has nothing of theirs to restore (gate fixes 4, finding 9; `docs/design/product-chat.md`'s "Scroll landing" bullet and `specs/001-core-session-workspace/spec.md`'s matching Clarification carry the full reasoning).
- Agents panel (#490): **always available, closed by default** when idle; **each Session's status is the group header, subagents are the rows**.
- **Icon policy by role** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, #474 grill item 1): three treatments — decision buttons in cards/dialogs/banners (Approve, Deny, Recover, Delete, Cancel) are **label only**; repeated row/toolbar actions (history, refresh, row menus) are **icon only** with a tooltip, accessible name and 24px minimum target; standalone create/open/copy commands (New conversation, Add repository, Open in file explorer, Copy path) get a **leading icon plus label**.
- **Icon-only requirements** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, item 2): strict bundle — Tooltip text equals the `aria-label` (verb first, sentence case), the Kbd shortcut shown when one exists, the tooltip dismissible with Esc, hoverable and persistent (WCAG 1.4.13), and also shown on keyboard focus-visible, a 400 ms open delay (instant between neighbours), a 24px minimum target; destructive or ambiguous actions are never icon-only, carrying a label or living in a menu instead. Dialog and sheet Close follow the same bundle.
- **Icon set and style** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, item 3): **lucide-react** for every UI, status, action and tool icon; **@tabler/icons-react** only for GitHub/GitLab/Bitbucket brand marks; default stroke width everywhere, icons use `currentColor`; three sizes — 12px (`size-3`) in metadata/chips and `xs` buttons, 14px (`size-3.5`) for inline status/notices and `sm` buttons, 16px (`size-4`) default for buttons/menu items/headers.
- **Icon set and style — superseded** (Stuart, 2026-10-10): **supersedes the brand-marks-only limit** on item 3 above. `@tabler/icons-react` is now allowed whenever `lucide-react` does not have the icon we need, not limited to GitHub/GitLab/Bitbucket brand marks; the brand marks remain the first intended use (tracked by **#646**), and the dependency is added in the PR that first needs it.
- **Button hierarchy** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, item 4): quiet — `outline` is the normal button everywhere; `default` (filled) only for the single commit action in a dialog/form and first-run; `ghost` for icon-only toolbar/row actions; `destructive` only as the final confirm button inside a dialog, never at rest. Sizes: `sm` for cards/dialogs/panes, `xs` for dense rows, `icon-sm` (28px) / `icon-xs` (24px) for icon-only. `secondary` and `link` stay unused unless a later decision adds them. Settles #531.
- **Menus and context menus** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, item 5, #471): by menu type — action menus (session row, repository row, manage-session row, right-click context menus) are **text only**; destination/settings menus (Profile, Appearance, GitHub accounts, Approval mode) keep a **leading icon on every item**. Shared rules: the shortcut is right-aligned in a Kbd, groups are separator-split, the destructive item is last after a separator in the destructive colour, and it is all icons or none within one menu.
- **Naming and microcopy** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, item 6): full vocabulary — **Add** (bring an existing outside thing in), **New** (start a fresh Refract-owned thing), **Create** (confirm button of a creating form only), **Cancel** (abandon a pending choice or form), **Close** (dismiss a read-only view with nothing pending), **Dismiss** (hide a notice that may come back), **Remove** (take out without deleting data) versus **Delete** (permanent) versus **Archive** (reversible hide). Confirm buttons repeat the verb, never OK or Yes; tooltips are the bare verb.
- **Status and feedback** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, item 7): written-down channels — today's four session-state icons stay (brand mark working, hollow circle idle, bell needs-you, square stopped); one severity set (info, success, warning, error, each a distinct lucide shape plus the word, colour only reinforces); banner = Session/pane-scoped problem until fixed, toast = transient app-level result (about 5s auto-dismiss, errors never auto-dismiss), inline text = form fields, transcript notice = agent-originated news; loading = a spinner plus words, the brand-mark spinner means only that an agent is working.
- **Placeholder and "coming soon" content** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, item 7, settles the C1 demo-content call for #393): unbuilt features are hidden, never shown disabled or "Coming soon". The FDB Knowledge and Agent Builder panes may show sample content only behind a **"Show sample data" Settings switch** (off by default); every sample element carries a **"Sample data" badge**, never mixed with real data, removed when #391/#392 land.
- **Layout and density** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, item 8, #360/#473/#393/#394): medium density kept; 4px grid with five allowed steps (4, 8, 12, 16, 24), no arbitrary pixel values in components; control heights follow the button sizes (24, 28, 32); transcript rhythm unchanged (24px between turns, 4px inside a bookkeeping run); one panel-header pattern for every panel/pane (fixed-height row, measured 40px against today's chat header, adopted, title left, ghost icon-only actions right, soft divider below).
- **Tokens and theming** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, item 9 / B4, #456/#458): three layers — palette (raw colour, theme-only), semantic roles (background, foreground, surface 1-3, border, accent, state-*, severity-*), component tokens (default to a semantic role). Components use only layers 2-3: no hex literals, no Tailwind palette colours, no opacity modifiers; to be enforced by a script beside `check:ui-text` and the contrast scripts (not built yet).
- **Custom themes** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, item 9, #458): theme files (JSON of palette, semantic and component token overrides) loaded from the user profile folder, listed beside the built-in presets, with import and export and a validity and contrast check on load; no in-app colour editor. Needs an amendment to spec 007 (`specs/007-app-themes/spec.md:17, 269` currently says presets only).
- **Surface ladder and edges** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, item 9, #550): depth from a surface ladder (background, then surface 1-3, each a step lighter); control edges (inputs, outline buttons, toggles, focus ring) at least 3:1; decorative dividers are soft, carry no meaning and have no contrast requirement. No grill comment addressed #452 directly; this rule may answer #452's open "reduce by 50%" question **[inference, confirm with Stuart]** — #452 stays open until he confirms.
- **Accent and primary fill** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, item 9, #549/#550): indigo family for the filled commit button and the accent role; accent used as text/links/focus takes a lighter indigo token. **Corrected contrast (measured unrounded):** white on `#6366F1` is **4.467:1, which fails the 4.5:1 normal-text threshold** (an earlier draft and #549 wrongly rounded this to 4.5:1); passing candidates include white on `#5A5EEE` (4.92:1), `#5B5FEF` (4.85:1), `#6064F0` (4.59:1) — #550 chooses the exact value.
- **Canvas colour** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, item 9, row B6, #549/#550): both ship as built-in presets, **Dark stays the default for now**. Dark keeps the 2026-10-07 retune (`#1a1a1a` to `#1e1e1e`); a new **Obsidian** preset uses the mock's `#0F1014`. Both must pass the contrast script; the composer stays strictly darker than the page background on either canvas.
- **Elevation and shadows** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, item 9, #549 mock review): two levels — in-flow surfaces (cards, panes, rows, the composer) get **no shadow** (depth is the surface ladder); floating layers (menus, popovers, tooltips, dialogs, toasts) use the highest surface tone, a soft edge, and one shared shadow token.
- **Micro-labels and type roles** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, items 6 and 10, #549 mock review, #4): no UPPERCASE tracked labels — section and group labels are sentence case, small, medium weight, muted, in the App font; monospace (the Code font) is for code-shaped data only (code, paths, commands, branch names, ids, hashes, aligned counts), never general metadata or labels.
- **Decorative colour and emoji** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, item 9, #549 mock review): colour is meaning, not decoration — icons are `currentColor`; colour appears only for state, severity, brand marks, syntax highlighting and diffs; no per-tab accent colours and no emoji as UI icons anywhere, including the file explorer (#463). **Amended 2026-10-15 (Stuart, #887 grill): a colour the developer chooses themselves for a Project counts as meaning (identity), so #887's Project header colour is allowed; the app still never adds decorative colour on its own.** Written into `docs/design/foundations-icons.md` ("Colour and decoration") the same day.
- **Zero-box** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, items 8/9, #556, #549 mock review): a box (fill or border) is allowed only where it marks something interactive or needing a hard boundary (the composer, decision cards, dialogs and floating layers, code blocks, inline code, diffs and tables, rendered diagrams, attachments/chips, and the user-message bubble exception); everything else is an unboxed row; no box inside a box. **#556 zero-box sweep built 2026-10-16, draft PR #944 (Closes #556)**: unboxed `NoticeRow.tsx`'s long-notice expansion and `ChatPane.tsx`'s Start-failure details `<pre>` (both previously `bg-muted p-2 rounded-md`), plus (found in gate round 1, fixed in round 2) the decision cards' own Alert-inside-Card nesting (approval, question) -- three violations found on the chat surface, all fixed.
- **Composer tone replaced** (Stuart, 2026-10-16, #927 grill): in Dark and Obsidian the composer is transparent with a faint border, ghost controls and a muted focus outline, like GitHub Copilot's; Dark (high contrast) keeps 3:1 edges and the full ring. The WCAG 1.4.11 departure is accepted and recorded in `docs/design/accessibility.md` "Accepted departures". Replaces the 2026-10-04 "strictly darker than the page" rule.
- **Reusable UI components only** (Stuart, 2026-10-15, #934 grill, ADR 0003): "All UI elements should be re-usable components, enhanced when use cases add complexity." New and touched UI uses shared components in `src/renderer/components/`, extended with props or slots, never copied or restyled in a feature folder; reviewers block new one-offs; existing ones are fixed one group per PR from #935. Written into `AGENTS.md` and `docs/design/components.md` ("Reusable components first").
- **Green for finished agents and Stages** (Stuart, 2026-10-15, #941 grill): finished agents and Stages show a green check and green text (success token) in the Subagent chain, Stage rows and Subagents panel; tool rows and groups keep the neutral check. Amends the #570/#563 "neutral done" rule; written into `docs/design/foundations-icons.md` ("Chat Status icons").
- **High contrast** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, items 7 and 9, #552): Dark (high contrast) keeps all state and severity colours and glyphs but raises every value — text 7:1, every edge including dividers 4.5:1 (soft dividers switched off), a thicker 2px focus ring, borders carry depth instead of tone. The Terminal Monochrome (glyph-first, minimal hue) treatment is **not adopted**; #552 can close once this is written into the design document.
- **Motion and focus** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, item 11, #566): functional motion plus the richer #566 animation set — hover and state fades up to 150ms, panel open and close up to 200ms, one spinner language for waiting, OS reduced motion removes transitions and replaces spinners with a static glyph plus the word; a 2px focus-visible ring (**amended 2026-10-09: achromatic `--ring`, not the accent token -- see the new Decisions bullet below and "Accent and primary fill" in foundations-tokens-theming.md**) at least 3:1, never removed, focus returns to the trigger after menus and dialogs. Which animations: see the next bullet (all five adopted).
- **Governance** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, item 12, #474 open questions): `docs/DESIGN.md` splits into **`docs/design/`** — an index plus pages for foundations, components, patterns and accessibility; `CONTEXT.md` gets the UI vocabulary (pill, chip, row, card, panel, pane, tab, readout) as a glossary only. Compliance via a design-system checklist in the Spec Kit plan gate plus lint scripts added over time (palette colours, alpha modifiers, icon-only controls without a Tooltip). One sweep issue (#579) lists every violation ordered by visibility.
- **Reversible removals** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, closes the open edge of item 2): may stay icon-only with the full Q2 bundle (Tooltip, `aria-label`, 24px target) and no confirm. "Destructive" means losing work that cannot be recovered (Delete session, Delete repository data, Discard draft) — those are never icon-only. Remove attachment and Remove queued message are reversible, so they stay icon-only but need the Tooltip they lack today.
- **Waiting indicator and animations** (Stuart, 2026-10-08, item 11, resolves the #566 follow-up): waiting gets an hourglass glyph plus a calm three-dot animation naming the target ("Waiting for 2 subagents"); reduced motion shows a static hourglass plus the words. **Adopted (all five #566 candidates):** a 150ms fade-in for new log items, a completion check that settles (about 200ms), a subtle shimmer on a running subagent row, a smooth panel collapse and expand (200ms, no chat-pane size or scroll change), and a streaming caret while the answer is written (static under reduced motion).
- **OMP minimum supported version** (Stuart, 2026-10-09, #520/B5): an `omp/18.8.0`-only verification is sufficient to set the new minimum supported version; **18.6.3** stands as the floor (per the 2026-10-07 native-`btw` decision), with the already-recorded caveat that only 18.8.0 itself was directly probed -- no 18.6.3/18.7.x binary needs to be obtained before this ships.
- **Focus ring colour — achromatic, decided** (Stuart, 2026-10-09, #550 follow-up): the focus ring (`--ring`) stays achromatic grey in every preset, including Obsidian. This settles the third item #550 had flagged as a declared deviation: accent now explicitly covers fills (the commit button, the accent role) and text/links (via `--accent-text`) only, never the focus ring. Contrast is already verified by `scripts/check-theme-contrast.mjs`'s `ring`/`background`, `ring`/`popover` and `ring`/`muted` pairs; no further work needed. This amends the 2026-10-08 "Motion and focus" bullet above, which had called for an accent-token ring. The separate focus-ring **width** question (2px vs 3px) stays open.
- **Closures (Stuart, 2026-10-10):** #648 composer-hint dropped icons-only + tooltip tier -- accepted the two-stage design as final, no further build. #6 (native-commands/Ctrl+N epic) and #618 (session-start polish) both closed: #6's remaining shortcuts (Ctrl+Shift+N, Ctrl+1 to Ctrl+9, Ctrl+Shift+A) are no longer tracked by any open issue; #618's known visual-only gaps are recorded in the PR body and #650.

- **Mockup stories review answers (Stuart, 2026-10-15, STORY-02 gap review):** a new pinned, non-modal **banner above the composer** lists running background (async) subagents with elapsed time and a link into the Agents panel; needs a new "status" feedback channel in `docs/design/patterns.md`, added as part of the same work (#865).
- **Ask card "Change" dropped (Stuart, 2026-10-15, STORY-03 gap review):** no "Change" action on an answered Ask card -- omp has no RPC to revoke an answer it has already received (#572).
- **#564 status line: no branch name or mode dot (Stuart, 2026-10-15, STORY-02/VISUAL-03 gap review):** left out of the composer's thin status line; the Approval pill moves into the composer box, and the Session row already shows the branch, so repeating either there would be redundant.
- **Composer restyle review follow-ups (Stuart, 2026-10-15, #877 UI architect review of #564):** four follow-ups filed and deferred -- #894 gives the composer box's control edge a `--composer-border` token with a contrast-gate pair (p3); #895 switches the Context usage "high" warning from `text-destructive` to the `severity-warning` role (p3); #896 renames the Readout Chip components to drop the retired "Chip" term (p3); #897 returns focus to the message field when Stop unmounts at the end of a turn instead of letting it fall to the page (p2, bug).
- **Grill first, then the full Spec Kit plan (Stuart, 2026-10-15):** every story starts with a `/grill-with-docs` session before any Spec Kit step runs, and the outcomes feed the Spec Kit steps; the issue's whole pickup plan then runs in order with no step skipped (converge: amend the spec, `/speckit.converge`, `/speckit.implement`; specify: specify, clarify, plan, tasks, analyze, implement). The grill is run by the main agent with Stuart, never a delegated worker; put this rule in every delegation brief.
- **Conversation history dialog truncation (Skippy, delegated by Stuart 2026-10-18, #439):** reconfirmed on current `origin/main` by the #862 live check -- `ConversationSwitcher.tsx` already marks the title (163) and preview (170) `truncate`, but the `ul`/`li`/row `button` have no `min-w-0` and `DialogContent` (139, `overflow-y-auto sm:max-w-md`) has no `overflow-x` handling, so the dialog still gets a horizontal scrollbar and a long preview clips mid-word. Decided: title and preview truncate to one line with an ellipsis (full text via the row's accessible name or a `title` attribute), and the dialog never shows a horizontal scrollbar at any width. Recorded on the issue (no new issue filed; folds into #439 per rule 8).
- **Priority review (Stuart, 2026-10-22):** p1 order set in "Queue > Priority order"; 10 issues closed and 32 relabelled (listed there). FDB integrations #391/#392 dropped to p3 while FDB discovery is outstanding.
- **P2 triage (Stuart, 2026-10-28):** 14 issues closed (4 done or duplicate, 10 spec verification chores folded into #650), 9 raised to p1, 57 lowered to p3, 7 narrowed, and epics #1271 (Worktree lifecycle), #1272 (Agents and subagents) and #1273 (Pull requests polish) created; #691 is the parent for OMP feature settings. Full lists under "Queue > Priority order".
- **UI polish is p1 (Stuart, 2026-10-28):** all `design`-labelled issues (Feats and ideas included) and all "UI polish" titled issues moved to p1, 90 issues. p1 is delivered in six waves of surface bundles (table under "Queue > Priority order").
- **Token cost and worker limits (Stuart, 2026-10-22):** OMP's `~/.omp/stats.db` showed October at ~$15.4k list price: cache reads $7.6k and cache writes $6.4k, output $1.4k; `earnest-stork` $11.8k (Sonnet 5 lane subagents averaging 396k tokens per call). The Advisor on Opus made 85% of premium requests (mostly 2026-10-01 to 10-06) but only 4% of token cost; it now runs on Gemini 4 Argon. Stuart's org is billed by tokens. Applied outside the repo (personal OMP and Copilot config, not repo files): `task.softRequestBudget` 200 to 80; `compaction.modelThresholds` caps Sonnet 5.5, Sonnet 5 and Haiku 5.5 at 120k tokens (`task.agentCompactionThresholdOverrides` is ignored in `--mode rpc`, so Refract relies on the per-model cap); the five quality-gate reviewers and `test-developer` moved from Opus (`@slow`) to Sonnet 5.5 (`@task`); the shared agent instructions gained a "Director model" rule. Working rule 13 is the repo-side copy.
- **#469 tool-row icons (Stuart's grill, 2026-10-26):** new spec 023 on the full Specify route (not a converge on 001). The tool icon leads each row and the status icon moves to the trailing slot; rows never show a raw tool id (it lives in the icon's tooltip, or muted after the label with the new Settings > Chat switch "Show tool names on rows", off by default); the label is the model's intent when given, else the verb from the one shared verb map, extended with an icon per tool; `bash` and `grep` rows now show a verb (supersedes spec 022's 2026-10-26 call); `wait` joins the map; MCP rows show Plug plus the humanised raw name, with the server › tool split deferred to #1226. Full record in `specs/023-tool-row-icons/spec.md` Clarifications.

Status update 2026-10-19: #1048 Holiday touches, framework plus Halloween **done**, draft PR #1053 (branch `feat/1048-holiday-touches`, `Refs #1048`), head `edcde895` after gate round 1 (process-tree CPU across every WebView2 descendant not just the root exe, bat stacking/redraw, real empty states, debug override hardening, the forbidden-new-Rust-test fix) and round 2 (a FAILING CPU budget check, item 2: median of 3 alternating Full/Artwork-only 60s samples asserted <=1.0s/60s above Artwork-only, plus a paused-state sample asserted <=0.1s/60s; `hasFocus()`/pointermove-throttle/modal-dialog pause gates; a single ambient-loop mount per window, not one per `ChatPane`; a computed-style z-index/isolation stacking proof replacing the unfalsifiable `elementFromPoint` one; wider scalloped bats (48x16, 3x span); em dashes; line-unpacking, `EmptyChatState.tsx` extracted to keep `App.tsx` under its own baseline). The budget caught a REAL regression: the round 1 `steps(26)` CSS animation measured 3.19s/60s (over budget) -- replaced with the ui-architect's own fallback design (static bats, one ~7s flight every 60-120s via `Element.animate()`, `document.getAnimations()` empty between flights), which then exposed a SECOND regression (the round 2 dialog-pause `MutationObserver` reacting to the whole app's DOM churn cost 0.156s/60s idle, over the paused budget) narrowed to `childList` on `document.body`'s direct children only (Radix portals dialogs there). Final budget: median active 0.328s/60s, paused -0.172s/60s (both well inside budget), refresh rate 62Hz. Pure calendar (all six windows), `Settings.holidayTouches` (Full/Artwork only/Off) end-to-end, a debug-only `debug.getOverrides` date/idle override (inert in release), Halloween across all five placements. Live-verified (`scripts/verify-1048-holiday-touches.mjs`, real built exe, real fake-omp turn, head `edcde895`): all 40 assertions PASS, `report.json` `dirty: false`, `failures: []`, `notExercised: []` -- all five theme presets, Full/Artwork only/Off, every pause rule (blur, hidden, 60s idle via shortened debug override, OS reduced motion), the CPU budget, the spinner's sr-only "Working" word and reduced-motion glyph, a no-override baseline, and the invalid-date-override live proof. `ci-local.mjs` 16/16 PASS (`cargo test` 731/731, `cargo clippy -D warnings` clean, `cargo fmt --check` clean). New-test gate (`git diff origin/main...HEAD -- src-tauri \| grep -cE "^\+\s*#\[(tokio::)?test\]"`) is 0. Bonfire Night (window opens 1 Nov), Christmas, New Year, Star Wars Day and Pi Day are NOT in this PR -- their `HolidayContent` entries exist in code but are unverified, and the three remaining ambient-loop effects (sparks/snow/sparks) are not built; tracked as this issue's own follow-up (specs/017-holiday-touches/tasks.md Phase 6).
Status update 2026-10-20: #1048 Holiday touches, gate rounds 3 and 4 **done** on draft PR #1053, final clean run head `4b363267`. Round 3 (High, item 1): a debug-only `REFRACT_HOLIDAY_FLIGHT_INTERVAL_MS` override (gated `#[cfg(debug_assertions)]`) so the CPU-budget sample reliably contains several flights, flight count asserted >=1, direct mid-flight/post-flight/blur-mid-flight/real-dialog-pause proofs, a round-trip flight (no snap), reactive reduced-motion/forced-colors cancellation, the shared `OVERLAY_OPEN_SELECTOR` covering every Radix overlay. Round 4 (Main's decision, after round 3's forced-flight-rate budget found about 0.86s of CPU per 7s flight -- WebView2's own frame-production cost for any running animation): flight duration 7s -> 4s (under WCAG 2.2.2's 5s threshold), gap 60-120s -> 90-180s, the animated/`will-change`'d target is now a wrapping `<div>` never the `<svg>` root, and an HONEST budget -- median per-flight CPU excess projected to the real worst-case production rate (`60 / (90s min gap + 4s duration)`), plus a separate hard per-flight ceiling (0.6s); paused-budget samples lengthened 60s -> 120s (normalised per 60s) to clear Windows' own CPU-tick quantization noise floor. Final measured budget: median per-flight excess 0.525s/flight (ceiling 0.6s), projected production-rate cost 0.663s/60s (budget 1.0s/60s), paused median 0.0078s/60s (budget 0.1s/60s). Three script bugs/flakes found and fixed live (falsified against the product first): a `pausedAtEnd` race reading right after the slow PowerShell CPU measurement; `document.getAnimations()` being document-wide (scoped to the loop's own subtree); a single keep-awake tap unable to outlast the debug-shortened idle timeout for the full debug-shortened flight gap (fixed with repeated taps), plus a genuine spec-defined gap between a cancelled Animation leaving `getAnimations()` and its `cancel` event actually firing. All 62 live-check assertions PASS, `report.json` `dirty: false`, `failures: []`, `notExercised: []`. `ci-local.mjs` all green, new-test gate 0.

Status update 2026-10-20 (later): #1039's draft PR #1094, post-draft self-review against the issue body's own grill decisions found 8 findings (`specs/007-app-themes/tasks.md` T044): Mermaid's own Cyber Amber palette was still the old brown literals (now reuses `DARK_FAMILY`); `--input`/`--scrollbar-thumb`/`--scrollbar-thumb-hover`/`--composer-focus`/`--ring`/`--sidebar-ring` were still independent values, not byte-identical to Default Dark's own (fixed, and several comments wrongly calling `--ring` part of the accent family corrected); FR-022 amended to the full surface+text+input+scrollbar+composer-focus wording; `verify-1043-theme-radio-helpers.mjs`'s own stale swatch and several stale Nordic-Frost/Cyber-Amber comment claims (`radio-group.tsx`, `theme-contrast-pairs.mjs`, `CommandPillOverlay.tsx`, `product-look-and-feel.md`'s "839/839") fixed; `verify-1039-amber-retune.mjs` extended with a real Dark-vs-Cyber-Amber token EQUALITY assertion. One finding flagged, not fixed: the new accent hue (55.9) closes Cyber Amber's own `--accent-text`/`--severity-error` gap to 1.23:1, under the 1.5:1 distinctness floor -- no single `--severity-error` lightness clears both that floor and the error-text-on-`--muted` floor at once, so a decision is needed (chroma change, a relaxed floor, or an accepted gap); left unchanged pending it. `check-theme-contrast` still 872/872, `pnpm typecheck` clean; the new assertion itself awaits the next build slot to live-run.
Status update 2026-10-20 (final): Main decided the flagged `--severity-error` trade-off -- a grid search (L 0.655-0.675, chroma 0.15-0.24, hue 5-25) found no point clearing both the 1.5:1 accent-vs-error floor and the error-text->=4.5:1-everywhere floor (confirmed, no overlap in that space), so WCAG text contrast wins: `--severity-error` retuned to `oklch(0.665 0.12 22)` (best achievable 1.42:1 swatch ratio, every text pair still >=4.5:1 including a real `destructive/80`-composited-hover-fill check that running `check-theme-contrast.mjs` for real surfaced, 4.33:1 -> 4.50:1), a named accepted departure for Cyber Amber only (`docs/design/accessibility.md`), `scripts/verify-551-presets.mjs`'s own floor lowered to 1.4. Three more round-1 items landed (`specs/007-app-themes/tasks.md` T044 items 9-11): a real Mermaid-render step (the existing `997-notice-severity` fixture extended with one assistant Mermaid message, not a new fixture) asserting the rendered SVG's node fill/text equal Dark's own; three more screenshots (a code block, an open dropdown, a toast) with a `settleForScreenshot` tooltip/hover-card guard before every shot that fails fatally if one is still open; PR #1094's own body corrected (dropped a stale "active Settings row outline" claim, noted the ring stays achromatic, noted #1070 will add the Settings swatch chip). `check-theme-contrast` 872/872, every no-build check green; the extended live script awaits the next build slot.
Status update 2026-10-20 (build slot, superseded below): #1039's draft PR #1094 live-verified on an earlier build slot. Merged `origin/main` first (#464 Git changes panel, no conflicts). `scripts/verify-1039-amber-retune.mjs` found two real bugs the live run falsified (not flakes): a hardcoded Mermaid node-fill literal that Mermaid never emits verbatim (replaced with cross-theme hex-colour-SET equality, same pattern as the token equality check) and an ambiguous `button[aria-expanded="true"]` toggle selector whose own "hide" click never actually closed the code-block panel, leaking the leftover Mermaid-source-open state (confirmed visually in a screenshot) into Cyber Amber's own turn -- fixed with a selector scoped to this specific toggle's own accessible name. A third bug (the slash-command refusal toast's draft leaking into later shots via `Input.insertText`-only-inserts-at-caret, FR-019) was fixed with a native `<textarea>` value-setter clear. Run: gitHead `64c73f84`, dirty `false`, 0 failures, 0 notExercised. `scripts/verify-551-presets.mjs`: **0 failures** -- confirms the retuned `--severity-error` clears the lowered 1.4:1 floor live (measured 1.42:1) and Nordic Frost/Dark (high contrast) are unaffected. Self-review after this run (T044, 11 findings, see below) found the hex-colour-SET check itself was too weak (it could pass even if Cyber Amber's palette had drifted, since colour SETS can coincidentally match) and the PR body's own "all text pixel-identical, only accent-coloured elements differ" claim was wrong (the severity-warning/error hues are deliberately retuned per-theme too) -- both corrected in the next build slot below.
Status update 2026-10-20 (build slot, final): origin/main had moved one commit (`docs(gate)` review-gate planning docs, no conflict) merged to `08cbdce0`. First attempt hit a real linker failure (`link.exe` LNK1327, `mt.exe` failed to parse another worktree's `windows-app-manifest.xml` -- the shared `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` had a stale path baked into a cached artifact from `feat-1077-agent-goal`); `cargo clean -p refract-tauri` (7.0 GiB) cleared it, rebuild succeeded. `scripts/verify-1039-amber-retune.mjs` then found what looked like a real Mermaid-palette-drift FAILURE (the round-2 full-SVG-equality check from the row above); falsified before accepting it: a scratch diagnostic diff showed the ONLY difference was `MermaidBlock`'s own `id="mermaid-${reactId}"` (`Markdown.tsx`, `React.useId()`), which is per-MOUNT, not per-palette -- this check's own screenshot section remounts the chat view (Manage sessions/Settings Appearance and back) between Dark's capture and Cyber Amber's comparison, so the id substring legitimately differs even though the content is identical (confirmed: equal lengths, single diff region, exactly that substring, e.g. `mermaid-_r_30_` vs `mermaid-_r_9m_`; after normalizing it out, the two SVGs are byte-for-byte equal). Fixed properly: `normalizeMermaidId` strips the volatile substring from both sides before comparing, so the check still catches a real palette drift. Committed `898a0797`; final clean run (dirty false) against that HEAD: `scripts/verify-1039-amber-retune.mjs` **0 failures, 0 notExercised**; `scripts/verify-551-presets.mjs` **0 failures, 0 notExercised**; `scripts/verify-997-notice-severity.mjs` all PASS, 0 FAIL. Screenshots viewed directly: the settled Cyber Amber Profile dropdown matches Dark's own chrome exactly (pure neutral dark grey, no stray tint), orange only on the Send button (`--send`). The Cyber Amber error notice (`oklch(0.665 0.12 22)`, `#d37472`) reads as a softened, dusty coral-red next to Dark's own more saturated red -- same hue family (22 degrees, matching Dark), clearly distinct from both the golden warning above it and the vivid orange accent, but visibly softer/pinker than a punchy "red" due to the WCAG-driven chroma cut (the documented accepted departure). PR #1094's own body corrected: the "all text pixel-identical, only accent-coloured elements differ" claim was wrong (the severity-warning/error hues are deliberately retuned per-theme too) -- fixed before this run. Round 3 review (code reviewer) caught two further PR-body overclaims, verified against source before fixing: the checked Settings theme-radio row is `bg-muted` (`ThemePicker.tsx:76`), a plain neutral grey identical in both themes, NOT `--primary`-tinted as an earlier edit wrongly claimed; and the Settings Save button/Background-watermark toggle use `--primary` (near-white, `#e5e5e5`, in Dark) via `Button`'s `default` variant and `Switch`'s checked state, not indigo -- the Send button itself genuinely IS indigo (`--send`, `#6d71f0`), confirmed by both a live DOM read and the screenshot pixels, so that part of the original claim was correct and kept. `ci-local.mjs` 16/16 PASS throughout (typecheck, vitest 148/148, indent, theme-contrast 872/872, Rust tests, clippy, Tauri debug build, UI text).
